1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The automata variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 188 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 211 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on automata structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 188. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 211. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a automata algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 426. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 409/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 426 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the automata structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 409. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
